NOT stored (ADR-2, bead bf-1pkfp):\n142\t    //\n143\t    // **ROOT CAUSE:** Prior to this fix, `content` was stored (TEXT | STORED),\n144\t    // duplicating the full conversation text already present in\n145\t    // `sessions/<plugin>/<id>.jsonl`. At a measured corpus of 13.3GB raw logs,\n146\t    // this caused the index to grow to 76GB by storing the same text twice.\n147\t    //\n148\t    // **THE FIX:** The `content` field is now indexed-only (TEXT, not STORED).\n149\t    // The normalized JSONL sessions remain the source of truth. Consumers that\n150\t    // need the raw text call `load_session_content()` to re-read from JSONL.\n151\t    //\n152\t    // **WHY THIS IS SAFE:** The JSONL files are already the durable copy — see\n153\t    // plan.md's Data Directory Layout. Storing them a second time in the index\n154\t    // provided no redundancy benefit, only storage overhead.\n155\t    //\n156\t    // **EXCEPTIONS:** `summary`, `solution_summary`, and `code_content` remain\n157\t    // STORED — these are short display fields or small code artifacts, not\n158\t    // full conversations. They don't contribute significantly to index growth.\n159\t    let content = builder.add_text_field(\"content\", TEXT);\n160\t    let summary = builder.add_text_field(\"summary\", TEXT | STORED);\n161\t    let solution_summary = builder.add_text_field(\"solution_summary\", TEXT | STORED);\n162\t    let code_content = builder.add_text_field(\"code_content\", TEXT | STORED);\n163\t\n164\t    // Exact match + faceted filtering\n165\t    let session_id = builder.add_text_field(\"session_id\", STRING | STORED);\n166\t    let source_agent = builder.add_text_field(\"source_agent\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n167\t    let project = builder.add_text_field(\"project\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n168\t    let tags = builder.add_text_field(\"tags\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n169\t    let outcome = builder.add_text_field(\"outcome\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n170\t    let error_fingerprint = builder.add_text_field(\"error_fingerprint\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n171\t    let file_paths = builder.add_text_field(\"file_paths\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n172\t    let git_commits = builder.add_text_field(\"git_commits\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n173\t    let doc_type = builder.add_text_field(\"doc_type\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n174\t\n175\t    // Code artifact fields\n176\t    let code_language = builder.add_text_field(\"code_language\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n177\t    let code_file_path = builder.add_text_field(\"code_file_path\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n178\t    let code_is_final = builder.add_bool_field(\"code_is_final\", STORED | FAST);\n179\t\n180\t    // Analytics + classification\n181\t    let model = builder.add_text_field(\"model\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n182\t    let session_type = builder.add_text_field(\"session_type\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n183\t    let parent_session_id = builder.add_text_field(\"parent_session_id\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n184\t\n185\t    // Date + numeric for range queries\n186\t    let timestamp = builder.add_date_field(\"timestamp\", INDEXED | STORED | FAST);\n187\t    let turn_count = builder.add_u64_field(\"turn_count\", INDEXED | STORED | FAST);\n188\t\n189\t    let schema = builder.build();\n190\t\n191\t    let fields = IndexFields {\n192\t        content,\n193\t model,\n209\t        session_type,\n210\t        parent_session_id,\n211\t        timestamp,\n212\t        turn_count,\n213\t    };\n214\t\n215\t    (schema, fields)\n216\t}\n217\t\n218\t/// Truncate tool_result content to TOOL_RESULT_MAX_CHARS.\n219\tfn truncate_tool_result(content: &str) -> &str {\n220\t    if content.len() > TOOL_RESULT_MAX_CHARS {\n221\t        // Find a char boundary near the limit\n222\t        let mut end = TOOL_RESULT_MAX_CHARS;\n223\t        while end > 0 && !content.is_char_boundary(end) {\n224\t            end -= 1;\n225\t        }\n226\t        debug!(\n227\t            original_len = content.len(),\n228\t            truncated_len = end,\n229\t            \"truncated tool_result content\"\n230\t        );\n231\t        &content[..end]\n232\t    } else {\n233\t        content\n234\t    }\n235\t}\n236\t\n237\t/// Trim content to CONTENT_MAX_BYTES by keeping first and last halves.\n238\tfn trim_middle(content: String) -> String {\n239\t    let bytes = content.len();\n240\t    if bytes <= CONTENT_MAX_BYTES {\n241\t        return content;\n242\t    }\n243\t\n244\t    debug!(\n245\t        original_bytes = bytes,\n246\t        max_bytes = CONTENT_MAX_BYTES,\n247\t        \"trimming document content (keep first+last halves)\"\n248\t    );\n249\t\n250\t    // Find byte boundary near the half point\n251\t    let mut mid = CONTENT_HALF_BYTES;\n252\t    while mid > 0 && !content.is_char_boundary(mid) {\n253\t        mid -= 1;\n254\t    }\n255\t\n256\t    let mut end = bytes - (CONTENT_MAX_BYTES - mid);\n257\t    while end > 0 && !content.is_char_boundary(end) {\n258\t        end -= 1;\n259\t    }\n260\t\n261\t    format!(\"{}\\n[...trimmed...]\\n{}\", &content[..mid], &content[end..])\n262\t}\n263\t\n264\t/// Build the content field from a list of events.\n265\t///\n266\t/// Concatenates events with role prefixes. Applies content truncation:\n267\t/// - user/assistant content: full\n268\t/// - tool_call content: full\n269\t/// - tool_result content: capped at 1000 chars\n270\t/// - Total capped at 500KB with trim-middle strategy\n271\t///\n272\t/// **ADR-2 CONTEXT (bead bf-1pkfp):** This function is `pub(crate)` specifically\n273\t/// so `load_session_content()` in `src/scraper/mod.rs` can rebuild this exact\n274\t/// string from a session's JSONL file at query time. Before ADR-2, the `content`\n275\t/// field was stored in Tantivy, so we could read it directly from the doc store.\n276\t/// Now that `content` is indexed-only, we re-read the JSONL file instead.\n277\t///\n278\t/// This preserves the exact same content format that would have been in the\n279\t/// stored field, ensuring that search snippets, more-like-this term extraction,\n280\t/// and analytics (cost estimation, problem-type classification) work identically\n281\t/// after ADR-2.\n282\tpub(crate) fn build_content(events: &[Event]) -> String {\n283\t    let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n284\t\n285\t    for event in events {\n286\t        let content = match event.role {\n287\t            Role::ToolResult => truncate_tool_result(&event.content).to_string(),\n288\t            _ => event.content.clone(),\n289\t        };\n290\t\n291\t        parts.push(format!(\"{}: {}\", event.role.as_str(), content));\n292\t    }\n293\t\n294\t    let concatenated = parts.join(\"\\n\\n\");\n295\t    trim_middle(concatenated)\n296\t}\n297\t\n298\t/// Classify a session into a type based on content classify_session_type(events: &[Event], manifest: &SessionManifest) -> &'static str {\n303\t    let content = build_content(events);\n304\t    let lower = content.to_lowercase();\n305\t    let        \"traceback\",\n314\t        \"panic\",\n315\t        \"segfault\",\n316\t        \"stack trace\",\n317\t        \"debug\",\n318\t    ];\n319\t    let feature_signals = [\n320\t        \"implement\",\n321\t        \"add feature\",\n322\t        \"create new\",\n323\t        \"build \",\n324\t        \"new module\",\n325\t        \"new function\",\n326\t        \"new endpoint\",\n327\t    ];\n328\t    let refactor_signals = [\n329\t        \"refactor\",\n330\t        \"cleanup\",\n331\t        \"clean up\",\n332\t        \"rename\",\n333\t        \"reorganize\",\n334\t        \"simplify\",\n335\t        \"extract method\",\n336\t    ];\n337\t    let config_signals = [\n338\t        \"config\",\n339\t        \"settings\",\n340\t        \"docker-compose\",\n341\t        \"yaml\",\n342\t        \"kubernetes\",\n343\t        \"deployment\",\n344\t        \"ci/cd\",\n345\t        \"github actions\",\n346\t    ];\n347\t    let ];\n355\t    let investigate_signals = [\n356\t        \"investigate\",\n357\t        \"explore\",\n358\t        \"understand\",\n359\t        \"why does\",\n360\t        \"what causes\",\n361\t        \"how does\",\n362\t    ];\n363\t\n364\t    let mut scores: [usize; 6] = [0; 6]; // debug, feature, refactor, config, docs, investigate\n365\t\n366\t    for s in &debug_signals {\n367\t        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n368\t            scores[0] += 2;\n369\t        }\n370\t    }\n371\t    for s in &feature_signals {\n372\t        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n373\t            scores[1] += &config_signals {\n382\t        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n383\t            scores[3] += 1;\n384\t        }\n385\t    }\n386\t    for s in &docs_signals {\n387\t        if lower_slice.contains(s) }\n395\t    }\n396\t\n397\t    // Outcome-based heuristics: unresolved errors → debug\n398\t    if manifest.outcome.as_deref() == Some(\"unresolved\") {\n399\t        scores[0] += 5;\n400\t    }\n401\t\n402\t    let types = [\n403\t        \"debug\",\n404\t        \"feature\",\n405\t        \"refactor\",\n406\t        \"configuration\",\n407\t        \"documentation\",\n408\t        \"investigation\",\n409\t    ];\n410\t    let max_idx = scores\n411\t        .iter()\n412\t        .enumerate()\n413\t        .max_by_key(|(_, &s)| s)\n414\t        .map(|(i, _)| i)\n415\t        .unwrap_or(0);\n416\t\n417\t    if scores[max_idx] == 0 {\n418\t        \"investigation\"\n419\t    } else {\n420\t        types[max_idx]\n421\t    }\n422\t}\n423\t\n424\t/// Load anti-pattern content from a session's sidecar file.\n425\t///\n426\t/// Returns formatted content with \"anti-pattern:\" prefixes for each pattern.\n427\tfn load_antipattern_content(data_dir: &Path, session_id: &str) -> continue;\n451\t        }\n452\t        if let Ok(pattern) =\n453\t            serde_json::from_str::<crate::enrichment::antipatterns::AntiPattern>(line)\n454\t        {\n455\t            // Format:  ));\n465\t                }\n466\t            }\n467\t        }\n468\t    }\n469\t\n470\t    if anti_pattern_lines.is_empty() {\n471\t        String::new()\n472\t    } else {\n473\t        anti_pattern_lines.join(\"\\n\\n\")\n474\t    }\n475\t}\n476\t\n477\t/// Build a Tantivy document for a session.\n478\t///\n479\t/// Takes a list of events belonging to the session and the session \"tantivy\";\n667\t\n668\t/// Manages incremental index updates during scraping.\n669\t///\n670\t/// The `IndexWriter` is held only during active scrape/commit operations,\n671\t/// allowing concurrent searches to proceed against the last committed state.\n672\tpub struct IndexManager {\n673\t    index: tantivy::Index,\n674\t    fields: IndexFields,\n675\t    writer: Option<tantivy::IndexWriter>,\n676\t    heap_size: usize,\n677\t    data_dir: std::path::PathBuf,\n678\t}\n679\t\n680\timpl IndexManager {\n681\t    /// Open an existing index or create a new one at `data_dir/index/tantivy`.\n682\t    pub fn open(data_dir: &Path) -> Result<Self> {\n683\t        let index_path = data_dir.join(\"index\").join(INDEX_DIR_NAME);\n684\t\n685\t        let (index, fields) = if index_path.exists() {\n686\t            let index = tantivy::Index::open_in_dir(&index_path)\n687\t                .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to open index: {}\", e)))?;\n688\t            // Derive field handles from the stored schema so IDs are always\n689\t            // in sync with whatever schema the index was originally created with.\n690\t            let fields = fields_from_schema(&index.schema());\n691\t            (index, fields)\n692\t        } else {\n693\t            let (schema, fields) = build_schema();\n694\t            std::fs::create_dir_all(&index_path)?;\n695\t            let index = tantivy::Index::create_in_dir(&index_path, schema)\n696\t                .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to create index: {}\", e)))?;\n697\t            (index, fields)\n698\t        };\n699\t\n700\t        Ok(IndexManager {\n701\t            index,\n702\t            fields,\n703\t            writer: None,\n704\t            heap_size: 50_000_000,\n705\t            data_dir: data_dir.to_path_buf(),\n706\t        })\n707\t    }\n708\t\n709\t    /// Set the Tantivy writer heap size in bytes.\n710\t    pub fn set_heap_size(&mut self, mb: usize) {\n711\t        self.heap_size = mb * 1_000_000;\n712\t    }\n713\t\n714\t    /// Begin a write session. Idempotent — no-op if writer is already active.\n715\t    ///\n716\t    /// The writer is created once and reused across multiple `index_session()`\n717\t    /// calls. It is held only during active scrape/commit, allowing concurrent\n718\t    /// searches to proceed against the last committed state.\n719\t    pub fn begin_write(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n720\t        if self.writer.is_some() {\n721\t            return Ok(());\n722\t        }\n723\t\n724\t        let writer = self.index.writer(self.heap_size).map_err(|e| {\n725\t            AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to create index writer: {}\", e))\n726\t        })?;\n727\t        self.writer = Some(writer);\n728\t        debug!(\"index writer opened\");\n729\t        Ok(())\n730\t    }\n731\t\n732\t    /// Index a session: delete any existing document for the session_id, then add the new one.\n733\t    ///\n734\t    /// The delete is soft until `commit()` is called, so within a single write session\n735\t    /// this correctly replaces the old document with the new one.\n736\t    pub fn index_session(&mut self, events: &[Event], manifest: &SessionManifest) -> Result<()> {\n737\t        let writer = self.writer.as_mut().ok_or_else(|| {\n738\t            AgentScribeError::DataDir(\n739\t                \"Index writer not active. Call begin_write() first.\".to_string(),\n740\t            )\n741\t        })?;\n742\t\n743\t        // Delete existing documents for this session_id (soft delete until commit)\n744\t        let term = Term::from_field_text(self.fields.session_id, &manifest.session_id);\n745\t        writer.delete_term(term);\n746\t        debug!(session_id = %manifest.session_id, \"deleted old index entry\");\n747\t\n748\t        // Load anti-pattern content from sidecar file\n749\t        let anti_pattern_content = load_antipattern_content(&self.data_dir, &manifest.session_id);\n750\t\n751\t        // Build and add new document\n752\t        let doc = build_session_document(&self.fields, events, manifest, &anti_pattern_content);\n753\t        writer\n754\t            .add_document(doc)\n755\t            .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to add document: {}\", e)))?;\n756\t        info!(session_id = %manifest.session_id, \"indexed session\");\n757\t\n758\t        Ok(())\n759\t    }\n760\t\n761\t    /// Delete all documents for a given session_id.\n762\t    pub fn delete_session(&mut self, session_id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n763\t        let writer = self.writer.as_mut().ok_or_else(|| {\n764\t            AgentScribeError::DataDir(\n765\t                \"Index writer not active. Call begin_write() first.\".to_string(),\n766\t            )\n767\t        })?;\n768\t\n769\t        let term = Term::from_field_text(self.fields.session_id, session_id);\n770\t        writer.delete_term(term);\n771\t        info!(session_id = %session_id, \"deleted session from index\");\n772\t        Ok(())\n773\t    }\n774\t\n775\t    /// Commit pending changes to make them visible to readers.\n776\t    ///\n777\t    /// Does NOT release the writer — subsequent calls to `index_session()` can\n778\t    /// continue using it. Call `finish()` to release the writer.\n779\t    pub fn commit(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n780\t        if let Some(ref mut writer) = self.writer {\n781\t            writer\n782\t                .commit()\n783\t                .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to commit index: {}\", e)))?;\n784\t            info!(\"index committed\");\n785\t        }\n786\t        Ok(())\n787\t    }\n788\t\n789\t    /// Commit pending changes and release the writer.\n790\t    ///\n791\t    /// After this call, a new `begin_write()` is required before further indexing.\n792\t    pub fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n793\t        self.commit()?;\n794\t        if self.writer.take().is_some() {\n795\t            debug!(\"index writer released\");\n796\t        }\n797\t        Ok(())\n798\t    }\n799\t\n800\t    /// Run garbage collection on the index to remove unused segment files.\n801\t    ///\n802\t    /// Opens a temporary writer to trigger GC, then releases it.\n803\t    pub fn optimize(&self) -> Result<()> {\n804\t        let writer: tantivy::IndexWriter = self.index.writer(50_000_000).map_err(|e| {\n805\t            AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to open writer for GC: {}\", e))\n806\t        })?;\n807\t        writer.garbage_collect_files().wait().map_err(|e| {\n808\t            AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to garbage collect index: {}\", e))\n809\t        })?;\n810\t        info!(\"index garbage collection complete\");\n811\t        Ok(())\n812\t    }\n813\t\n814\t    /// Check if a writer is currently active.\n815\t    #[allow(dead_code)]\n816\t    pub fn is_writing(&self) -> bool {\n817\t        self.writer.is_some()\n818\t    }\n819\t\n820\t    /// Get a reference to the underlying tantivy Index.\n821\t    #[allow(dead_code)]\n822\t    pub fn index(&self) -> &tantivy::Index {\n823\t        &self.index\n824\t    }\n825\t\n826\t    /// Get the schema for this index.\n827\t    #[allow(dead_code)]\n828\t    pub fn schema(&self) -> Schema {\n829\t        self.index.schema()\n830\t    }\n831\t}\n832\t\n833\t#[cfg(test)]\n834\tmod tests .get_field(\"turn_count\")\n            .expect(\"missing 'turn_count' field in schema\"),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Build the Tantivy schema with all fields from plan.md.\npub fn build_schema() -> (Schema, IndexFields) {\n    let mut builder = Schema::builder();\n\n    // Full-text searchable. NOT stored (ADR-2, bead bf-1pkfp):\n    //\n    // **ROOT CAUSE:** Prior to this fix, `content` was stored (TEXT | STORED),\n    // duplicating the full conversation text already present in\n    // `sessions/<plugin>/<id>.jsonl`. At a measured corpus of 13.3GB raw logs,\n    // this caused the index to grow to 76GB by storing the same text twice.\n    //\n    // **THE FIX:** The `content` field is now indexed-only (TEXT, not STORED).\n    // The normalized JSONL sessions remain the source of truth. Consumers that\n    // need the raw text call `load_session_content()` to re-read from JSONL.\n    //\n    // **WHY THIS IS SAFE:** The JSONL files are already the durable copy — see\n    // plan.md's Data Directory Layout. Storing them a second time in the index\n    // provided no redundancy benefit, only storage overhead.\n    //\n    // **EXCEPTIONS:** `summary`, `solution_summary`, and `code_content` remain\n    // STORED — these are short display fields or small code artifacts, not\n    // full conversations. They don't contribute significantly to index growth.\n    let content = builder.add_text_field(\"content\", TEXT);\n    let summary = builder.add_text_field(\"summary\", TEXT | STORED);\n    let solution_summary = builder.add_text_field(\"solution_summary\", TEXT | STORED);\n    let code_content = builder.add_text_field(\"code_content\", TEXT | STORED);\n\n    // Exact match + faceted filtering\n    let session_id = builder.add_text_field(\"session_id\", STRING | STORED);\n    let source_agent = builder.add_text_field(\"source_agent\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let project = builder.add_text_field(\"project\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let tags = builder.add_text_field(\"tags\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let outcome = builder.add_text_field(\"outcome\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let error_fingerprint = builder.add_text_field(\"error_fingerprint\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let file_paths = builder.add_text_field(\"file_paths\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let git_commits = builder.add_text_field(\"git_commits\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let doc_type = builder.add_text_field(\"doc_type\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n\n    // Code artifact let code_is_final = builder.add_bool_field(\"code_is_final\", STORED | FAST);\n\n    // Analytics + classification\n    let model = builder.add_text_field(\"model\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let session_type = builder.add_text_field(\"session_type\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n    let parent_session_id = builder.add_text_field(\"parent_session_id\", STRING | STORED | FAST);\n\n    // Date +    let schema = builder.build();\n\n    let fields = IndexFields {\n        content,\n        summary,\n        solution_summary,\n        code_content,\n        session_id,\n        source_agent,\n TOOL_RESULT_MAX_CHARS.\nfn truncate_tool_result(content: &str) -> &str {\n    if content.len() > TOOL_RESULT_MAX_CHARS {\n        // Find a char boundary near the limit\n        let mut end = TOOL_RESULT_MAX_CHARS;\n        while end > 0 && !content.is_char_boundary(end) {\n            end -= 1;\n        }\n        debug!(\n            original_len = content.len(),\n            truncated_len = end,\n            \"truncated tool_result content\"\n        );\n        &content[..end]\n    } else {\n        content\n    }\n}\n\n/// Trim content to CONTENT_MAX_BYTES by keeping first and last halves.\nfn trim_middle(content: String) -> String {\n    let bytes = content.len();\n    if bytes <= CONTENT_MAX_BYTES {\n        return content;\n    }\n\n    debug!(\n        original_bytes = bytes,\n        max_bytes = CONTENT_MAX_BYTES,\n        \"trimming document content (keep first+last halves)\"\n    );\n\n    // Find byte boundary near the half point\n    let mut mid = CONTENT_HALF_BYTES;\n    while mid > 0 && !content.is_char_boundary(mid) {\n        mid -= 1;\n    }\n\n    let mut end = bytes - (CONTENT_MAX_BYTES - mid);\n    while end > 0 && !content.is_char_boundary(end) {\n        end -= 1;\n    }\n\n    format!(\"{}\\n[...trimmed...]\\n{}\", &content[..mid], &content[end..])\n}\n\n/// Build the content field from a list of events.\n///\n/// Concatenates events with role prefixes. Applies content truncation:\n/// - user/assistant content: full\n/// - tool_call content: full\n/// - tool_result content: capped at 1000 chars\n/// - Total capped at 500KB with trim-middle strategy\n///\n/// **ADR-2 CONTEXT (bead bf-1pkfp):** This function is `pub(crate)` specifically\n/// so `load_session_content()` in `src/scraper/mod.rs` can rebuild this exact\n/// string from a session's JSONL file at query time. Before ADR-2, the `content`\n/// field was stored in Tantivy, so we could read it directly from the doc store.\n/// Now that `content` is indexed-only, we re-read the JSONL file instead.\n///\n/// This preserves the exact same content format that would have been in the\n/// stored field, ensuring that search snippets, more-like-this term extraction,\n/// and analytics (cost estimation, problem-type classification) work identically\n/// after ADR-2.\npub(crate) fn build_content(events: &[Event]) -> String {\n    let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n\n    for event in events {\n        let content = match event.role {\n            Role::ToolResult => truncate_tool_result(&event.content).to_string(),\n            _ => event.content.clone(),\n        };\n\n        parts.push(format!(\"{}: {}\", event.role.as_str(), content));\n    }\n\n    let concatenated = parts.join(\"\\n\\n\");\n    trim_middle(concatenated)\n}\n\n/// Classify a session into a type based on content signals.\n///\n/// Uses keyword matching on the concatenated content to assign one of:\n/// debug, feature, refactor, investigation, configuration, documentation.\nfn classify_session_type(events: &[Event], manifest: &SessionManifest) -> &'static str {\n    let content = build_content(events);\n    let lower = content.to_lowercase();\n    let lower_slice = lower.as_str();\n\n    let debug_signals = [\n        \"error\",\n        \"bug\",\n        \"fix\",\n        \"crash\",\n        \"exception\",\n        \"traceback\",\n        \"panic\",\n        \"segfault\",\n        \"stack trace\",\n        \"debug\",\n    ];\n    let feature_signals = [\n        \"implement\",\n        \"add feature\",\n        \"create new\",\n        \"build \",\n        \"new module\",\n        \"new function\",\n        \"new endpoint\",\n    ];\n    let refactor_signals = [\n        \"refactor\",\n        \"cleanup\",\n        \"clean up\",\n        \"rename\",\n        \"reorganize\",\n        \"simplify\",\n        \"extract method\",\n    ];\n    let config_signals = [\n        \"config\",\n        \"settings\",\n        \"docker-compose\",\n        \"yaml\",\n        \"kubernetes\",\n        \"deployment\",\n        \"ci/cd\",\n        \"github actions\",\n    ];\n    let docs_signals = [\n        \"readme\",\n        \"documentation\",\n        \"docs\",\n        \"comment\",\n        \"docstring\",\n        \"changelog\",\n    ];\n    let investigate_signals = [\n        \"investigate\",\n        \"explore\",\n        \"understand\",\n        \"why does\",\n        \"what causes\",\n        \"how does\",\n    ];\n\n    let mut scores: [usize; 6] = [0; 6]; // debug, feature, refactor, config, docs, investigate\n\n    for s in &debug_signals {\n        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n            scores[0] += 2;\n        }\n    }\n    for s in &feature_signals {\n        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n            scores[1] += 1;\n        }\n    }\n    for s in &refactor_signals {\n        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n            scores[2] += 1;\n        }\n    }\n    for s in &config_signals {\n        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n            scores[3] += 1;\n        }\n    }\n    for s in &docs_signals {\n        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n            scores[4] += 1;\n        }\n    }\n    for s in &investigate_signals {\n        if lower_slice.contains(s) {\n            scores[5] += 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Outcome-based heuristics: unresolved errors → debug\n    if manifest.outcome.as_deref() == Some(\"unresolved\") {\n        scores[0] += 5;\n    }\n\n    let types = [\n        \"debug\",\n        \"feature\",\n        \"refactor\",\n        \"configuration\",\n        \"documentation\",\n        \"investigation\",\n    ];\n    let max_idx = scores\n        .iter()\n        .enumerate()\n        .max_by_key(|(_, &s)| s)\n        .map(|(i, _)| i)\n        .unwrap_or(0);\n\n    if scores[max_idx] == 0 {\n        \"investigation\"\n    } else {\n        types[max_idx]\n    }\n}\n\n/// Load anti-pattern content from a session's sidecar file.\n///\n/// Returns formatted content with \"anti-pattern:\" prefixes for each pattern.\nfn load_antipattern_content(data_dir: &Path, session_id: &str) -> String {\n    let sessions_dir = data_dir.join(\"sessions\");\n    let parts: Vec<&str> = session_id.splitn(2, '/').collect();\n    if parts.len() != 2 {\n        return String::new();\n    }\n\n    let sidecar_path = sessions_dir\n        .join(parts[0])\n        .join(format!(\"{}.anti-patterns.jsonl\", parts[1]));\n\n    if !sidecar_path.exists() {\n        return String::new();\n    }\n\n    let content = match std::fs::read_to_string(&sidecar_path) {\n        Ok(c) => c,\n        Err(_) => return String::new(),\n    };\n\n    let mut anti_pattern_lines = Vec::new();\n    for line in content.lines() {\n        if line.trim().is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if let Ok(pattern) =\n            serde_json::from_str::<crate::enrichment::antipatterns::AntiPattern>(line)\n        {\n            // Format: anti-pattern: <pattern description>\n            anti_pattern_lines.push(format!(\"anti-pattern: {}\", pattern.pattern));\n            // Include working alternatives if available\n            for (i, alt_summary) in pattern.working_alternative_summaries.iter().enumerate() {\n                if !alt_summary.is_empty() {\n                    anti_pattern_lines.push(format!(\n                        \"  working alternative {}: {}\",\n                        i + 1,\n                        alt_summary\n                    ));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if anti_pattern_lines.is_empty() {\n        String::new()\n    } else {\n        anti_pattern_lines.join(\"\\n\\n\")\n    }\n}\n\n/// Build a Tantivy document for a session.\n///\n/// Takes a list of events belonging to the session and the session manifest,\n/// and produces a single Tantivy document with the `doc_type` set to \"session\".\npub fn build_session_document(\n    fields: &IndexFields,\n    events: &[Event],\n    manifest: &SessionManifest,\n    anti_pattern_content: &str,\n) -> TantivyDocument {\n    let mut doc = TantivyDocument::new();\n\n    // Content field — concatenated conversation with role prefixes, plus anti-patterns\n    let mut content = build_content(events);\n    if !anti_pattern_content.is_empty() {\n        content.push_str(\"\\n\\n\");\n        content.push_str(anti_pattern_content);\n    }\n    doc.add_text(fields.content, &content);\n\n    // Summary fields\n    if let Some(ref summary) = manifest.summary {\n        doc.add_text(fields.summary, summary);\n    }\n\n    // Faceted fields\n    doc.add_text(fields.session_id, &manifest.session_id);\n    doc.add_text(fields.source_agent, &manifest.source_agent);\n    if let Some(ref project) = manifest.project {\n        doc.add_text(fields.project, project);\n    }\n\n    // Tags: merge manifest tags with auto-extracted tags, deduplicate\n    let mut all_tags: HashSet<String> = manifest.tags.iter().cloned().collect();\n    for tag in tags::extract_tags(events) {\n        all_tags.insert(tag);\n    }\n    for tag in &all_tags {\n        doc.add_text(fields.tags, tag);\n    }\n    if let Some(ref outcome) = manifest.outcome {\n        doc.add_text(fields.outcome, outcome);\n    }\n\n    // Collect unique error fingerprints across all events\n    let mut fingerprints: HashSet<&str> = HashSet::new();\n    for event in events {\n        for fp in &event.error_fingerprints {\n            fingerprints.insert(fp.as_str());\n        }\n    }\n    for fp in &fingerprints {\n        doc.add_text(fields.error_fingerprint, fp);\n    }\n\n    // Collect unique file paths from manifest + events\n    let mut all_files: HashSet<&str> = HashSet::new();\n    for f in &manifest.files_touched {\n        all_files.insert(f.as_str());\n    }\n    for event in events {\n        for f in &event.file_paths {\n            all_files.insert(f.as_str());\n        }\n    }\n    for f in &all_files {\n        doc.add_text(fields.file_paths, f);\n    }\n\n    // Doc type\n    doc.add_text(fields.doc_type, \"session\");\n\n    // Session type classification\n    let session_type = classify_session_type(events, manifest);\n    doc.add_text(fields.session_type, session_type);\n\n    // Model\n    if let Some(ref model) = manifest.model {\n        doc.add_text(fields.model, model);\n    }\n\n    // Parent session ID for subagent sessions\n    if let Some(ref parent_session_id) = manifest.parent_session_id {\n        doc.add_text(fields.parent_session_id, parent_session_id);\n    }\n\n    // Timestamp — use the session start time\n    let tantivy_ts = tantivy::DateTime::from_timestamp_secs(manifest.started.timestamp());\n    doc.add_date(fields.timestamp, tantivy_ts);\n\n    // Turn count\n    doc.add_u64(fields.turn_count, manifest.turns as u64);\n\n    doc\n}\n\n/// Build a Tantivy document for a code artifact.\n///\n/// Code artifacts share session-level fields with their parent session and\n/// have the `doc_type` set to \"code_artifact\".\n#[allow(dead_code)]\npub fn build_code_artifact_document(\n    fields: &IndexFields,\n    session_id: &str,\n    source_agent: &str,\n    project: Option<&str>,\n    timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,\n    code_language: &str,\n    code_file_path: &str,\n    code_content: &str,\n    code_is_final: bool,\n    model: Option<&str>,\n) -> TantivyDocument {\n    let mut doc = TantivyDocument::new();\n\n    // Content — code content goes into both content and code_content\n    doc.add_text(fields.content, code_content);\n    doc.add_text(fields.code_content, code_content);\n\n    // Session correlation fields\n    doc.add_text(fields.session_id, session_id);\n    doc.add_text(fields.source_agent, source_agent);\n    if let Some(project) = project {\n        doc.add_text(fields.project, project);\n    }\n\n    // Code artifact fields\n    doc.add_text(fields.code_language, code_language);\n    doc.add_text(fields.code_file_path, code_file_path);\n    doc.add_bool(fields.code_is_final, code_is_final);\n\n    // Doc type\n    doc.add_text(fields.doc_type, \"code_artifact\");\n\n    // Model\n    if let Some(model) = model {\n        doc.add_text(fields.model, model);\n    }\n\n    // Timestamp\n    let tantivy_ts = tantivy::DateTime::from_timestamp_secs(timestamp.timestamp());\n    doc.add_date(fields.timestamp, tantivy_ts);\n\n    doc\n}\n\n/// Build a minimal `SessionManifest` from scraped events for indexing.\n///\n/// This creates a manifest with metadata extracted directly from the events,\n/// without enrichment data (summary, outcome, etc.) which is added later.\npub fn build_manifest_from_events(\n    events: &[Event],\n    session_id: &str,\n    source_agent: &str,\n    project: Option<&str>,\n    model: Option<&str>,\n    parent_session_id: Option<&str>,\n) -> SessionManifest {\n    let started = events.first().map(|e| e.ts).unwrap_or_else(Utc::now);\n    let ended = events.last().map(|e| e.ts);\n    let turns = events.len() as u32;\n\n    let mut files_touched: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    let mut seen: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();\n    for event in events {\n        for f in &event.file_paths {\n            if seen.insert(f.clone()) {\n                files_touched.push(f.clone());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    SessionManifest {\n        session_id: session_id.to_string(),\n        source_agent: source_agent.to_string(),\n        project: project.map(|s| s.to_string()),\n        started,\n        ended,\n        turns,\n        summary: None,\n        outcome: None,\n        tags: Vec::new(),\n        files_touched,\n        model: model.map(|s| s.to_string()),\n        parent_session_id: parent_session_id.map(|s| s.to_string()),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Tantivy index directory name\npub const INDEX_DIR_NAME: &str = \"tantivy\";\n\n/// Manages incremental index updates during scraping.\n///\n/// The `IndexWriter` is held only during active scrape/commit operations,\n/// allowing concurrent searches to proceed against the last committed state.\npub struct IndexManager {\n    index: tantivy::Index,\n    fields: IndexFields,\n    writer: Option<tantivy::IndexWriter>,\n    heap_size: usize,\n    data_dir: std::path::PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl IndexManager {\n    /// Open an existing index or create a new one at `data_dir/index/tantivy`.\n    pub fn open(data_dir: &Path) -> Result<Self> {\n        let index_path = data_dir.join(\"index\").join(INDEX_DIR_NAME);\n\n        let (index, fields) = if index_path.exists() {\n            let index = tantivy::Index::open_in_dir(&index_path)\n                .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to open index: {}\", e)))?;\n            // Derive field handles from the stored schema so IDs are always\n            // in sync with whatever schema the index was originally created with.\n            let fields = fields_from_schema(&index.schema());\n            (index, fields)\n        } else {\n            let (schema, fields) = build_schema();\n            std::fs::create_dir_all(&index_path)?;\n            let index = tantivy::Index::create_in_dir(&index_path, schema)\n                .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to create index: {}\", e)))?;\n            (index, fields)\n        };\n\n        Ok(IndexManager {\n            index,\n            fields,\n            writer: None,\n            heap_size: 50_000_000,\n            data_dir: data_dir.to_path_buf(),\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Set the Tantivy writer heap size in bytes.\n    pub fn set_heap_size(&mut self, mb: usize) {\n        self.heap_size = mb * 1_000_000;\n    }\n\n    /// Begin a write session. Idempotent — no-op if writer is already active.\n    ///\n    /// The writer is created once and reused across multiple `index_session()`\n    /// calls. It is held only during active scrape/commit, allowing concurrent\n    /// searches to proceed against the last committed state.\n    pub fn begin_write(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n        if self.writer.is_some() {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n\n        let writer = self.index.writer(self.heap_size).map_err(|e| {\n            AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to create index writer: {}\", e))\n        })?;\n        self.writer = Some(writer);\n        debug!(\"index writer opened\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Index a session: delete any existing document for the session_id, then add the new one.\n    ///\n    /// The delete is soft until `commit()` is called, so within a single write session\n    /// this correctly replaces the old document with the new one.\n    pub fn index_session(&mut self, events: &[Event], manifest: &SessionManifest) -> Result<()> {\n        let writer = self.writer.as_mut().ok_or_else(|| {\n            AgentScribeError::DataDir(\n                \"Index writer not active. Call begin_write() first.\".to_string(),\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        // Delete existing documents for this session_id (soft delete until commit)\n        let term = Term::from_field_text(self.fields.session_id, &manifest.session_id);\n        writer.delete_term(term);\n        debug!(session_id = %manifest.session_id, \"deleted old index entry\");\n\n        // Load anti-pattern content from sidecar file\n        let anti_pattern_content = load_antipattern_content(&self.data_dir, &manifest.session_id);\n\n        // Build and add new document\n        let doc = build_session_document(&self.fields, events, manifest, &anti_pattern_content);\n        writer\n            .add_document(doc)\n            .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to add document: {}\", e)))?;\n        info!(session_id = %manifest.session_id, \"indexed session\");\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Delete all documents for a given session_id.\n    pub fn delete_session(&mut self, session_id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n        let writer = self.writer.as_mut().ok_or_else(|| {\n            AgentScribeError::DataDir(\n                \"Index writer not active. Call begin_write() first.\".to_string(),\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        let term = Term::from_field_text(self.fields.session_id, session_id);\n        writer.delete_term(term);\n        info!(session_id = %session_id, \"deleted session from index\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Commit pending changes to make them visible to readers.\n    ///\n    /// Does NOT release the writer — subsequent calls to `index_session()` can\n    /// continue using it. Call `finish()` to release the writer.\n    pub fn commit(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n        if let Some(ref mut writer) = self.writer {\n            writer\n                .commit()\n                .map_err(|e| AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to commit index: {}\", e)))?;\n            info!(\"index committed\");\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Commit pending changes and release the writer.\n    ///\n    /// After this call, a new `begin_write()` is required before further indexing.\n    pub fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n        self.commit()?;\n        if self.writer.take().is_some() {\n            debug!(\"index writer released\");\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Run garbage collection on the index to remove unused segment files.\n    ///\n    /// Opens a temporary writer to trigger GC, then releases it.\n    pub fn optimize(&self) -> Result<()> {\n        let writer: tantivy::IndexWriter = self.index.writer(50_000_000).map_err(|e| {\n            AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to open writer for GC: {}\", e))\n        })?;\n        writer.garbage_collect_files().wait().map_err(|e| {\n            AgentScribeError::DataDir(format!(\"Failed to garbage collect index: {}\", e))\n        })?;\n        info!(\"index garbage collection complete\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Check if a writer is currently active.\n    #[allow(dead_code)]\n    pub fn is_writing(&self) -> bool {\n        self.writer.is_some()\n    }\n\n    /// Get a reference to the underlying tantivy Index.\n    #[allow(dead_code)]\n    pub fn index(&self) -> &tantivy::Index {\n        &self.index\n    }\n\n    /// Get the schema for this index.\n    #[allow(dead_code)]\n    pub fn schema(&self) -> Schema {\n        self.index.schema()\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_schema_builds() {\n        let (schema, fields) = build_schema();\n\n        // Verify  assert!(schema.get_field(\"turn_count\").is_ok());\n        assert!(schema.get_field(\"doc_type\").is_ok());\n        assert!(schema.get_field(\"code_is_final\").is_ok());\n\n        // Verify field IndexManager::open(temp_dir.path()).unwrap();\n        assert!(!manager.is_writing());\n\n        manager.begin_write().unwrap();\n        assert!(manager.is_writing());\n\n        // Idempotent second call\n        manager.begin_write().unwrap();\n        assert!(manager.is_writing());\n\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let events = vec![Event::new(\n            now,\n            \"test/1\".to_string(),\n            \"claude\".to_string(),\n            Role::User,\n            \"fix the bug\".to_string(),\n        )];\n        let manifest = build_manifest_from_events(&events, \"test/1\", \"claude\", None, None, None);\n        manager.index_session(&events, &manifest).unwrap();\n\n        manager.commit().unwrap();\n        assert!(manager.is_writing()); // still active after commit\n\n        manager.finish().unwrap();\n        assert!(!manager.is_writing()); // released after finish\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_index_manager_index_without_writer_errors() {\n test_index_manager_reopen() {\n        use tempfile::TempDir;\n        let temp_dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n\n        // Create and write\n        {\n            let mut manager = IndexManager::open(temp_dir.path()).unwrap();\n            manager.begin_write().unwrap();\n            let now = Utc::now();\n            let events = vec![Event::new(\n                now,\n                \"test/1\".to_string(),\n                \"claude\".to_string(),\n                Role::User,\n                \"hello world\".to_string(),\n            )];\n            let manifest =\n                build_manifest_from_events(&events, \"test/1\", \"claude\", None, None, None);\n            manager.index_session(&events, &manifest).unwrap();\n            manager.finish().unwrap();\n        }\n\n        // Reopen — should not panic\n        let manager2 = IndexManager::open(temp_dir.path()).unwrap();\n        assert!(!manager2.is_writing());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_incremental_update_replaces_old_document() {\n        // Re-indexing a session must produce exactly one document with the new\n        // content, not duplicate the old one.\n        use tantivy::collector::Count;\n        use tantivy::query::TermQuery;\n        use tantivy::schema::IndexRecordOption;\n        use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n        let temp_dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let now = Utc::now();\n\n        // --- First index pass: original content ---\n        {\n            let mut manager = IndexManager::open(temp_dir.path()).unwrap();\n            manager.begin_write().unwrap();\n            let events = vec![\n                Event::new(\n                    now,\n                    \"test/abc\".to_string(),\n                    \"claude\".to_string(),\n                    Role::User,\n                    \"original query\".to_string(),\n                ),\n                Event::new(\n                    now,\n                    \"test/abc\".to_string(),\n                    \"claude\".to_string(),\n                    Role::Assistant,\n                    \"original answer\".to_string(),\n                ),\n            ];\n            let manifest =\n                build_manifest_from_events(&events, \"test/abc\", \"claude\", None, None, None);\n            manager.index_session(&events, &manifest).unwrap();\n            manager.finish().unwrap();\n        }\n\n        // --- Second index pass: updated content (simulates re-scrape) ---\n        {\n            let mut manager = IndexManager::open(temp_dir.path()).unwrap();\n            manager.begin_write().unwrap();\n            let events = vec![\n                Event::new(\n                    now,\n                    \"test/abc\".to_string(),\n                    \"claude\".to_string(),\n                    Role::User,\n                    \"updated query after edit\".to_string(),\n                ),\n                Event::new(\n                    now,\n                    \"test/abc\".to_string(),\n                    \"claude\".to_string(),\n                    Role::Assistant,\n                    \"updated answer\".to_string(),\n                ),\n            ];\n            let manifest =\n                build_manifest_from_events(&events, \"test/abc\", \"claude\", None, None, None);\n            manager.index_session(&events, &manifest).unwrap();\n            manager.finish().unwrap();\n        }\n\n        // --- Verify: exactly one document for session test/abc ---\n        let index_path = temp_dir.path().join(\"index\").join(INDEX_DIR_NAME);\n        let index = tantivy::Index::open_in_dir(&index_path).unwrap();\n        let fields = fields_from_schema(&index.schema());\n        let reader = index.reader().unwrap();\n        let searcher = reader.searcher();\n\n        let term = Term::from_field_text(fields.session_id, \"test/abc\");\n        let query = TermQuery::new(term, IndexRecordOption::Basic);\n        let count = searcher.search(&query, &Count).unwrap();\n\n        // Must be exactly 1 — delete_term + add ensures no duplicates\n        assert_eq!(\n            count, 1,\n            \"re-indexing a session must produce exactly one document\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_incremental_update_content_is_updated() {\n        // After re-indexing, the stored content must reflect the new events.\n        use tantivy::collector::TopDocs;\n        use tantivy::query::TermQuery;\n        use tantivy::schema::IndexRecordOption;\n        use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n        let temp_dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let now = Utc::now();\n\n        // First pass\n        {\n            let mut manager = IndexManager::open(temp_dir.path()).unwrap();\n            manager.begin_write().unwrap();\n            let events = vec![Event::new(\n                now,\n                \"test/xyz\".to_string(),\n                \"claude\".to_string(),\n                Role::User,\n                \"old content here\".to_string(),\n            )];\n            let manifest =\n                build_manifest_from_events(&events, \"test/xyz\", \"claude\", None, None, None);\n            manager.index_session(&events, &manifest).unwrap();\n            manager.finish().unwrap();\n        }\n\n        // Second pass with new content\n        {\n            let mut manager = IndexManager::open(temp_dir.path()).unwrap();\n            manager.begin_write().unwrap();\n            let
